<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掌握终端环境需要具备多步规划、反馈驱动执行和动态状态适应能力的语言智能体，但当前训练受限于依赖爬取的外部仓库，限制了领域多样性、环境可控性和针对性能力缺陷训练

Method: 引入零依赖合成管道，从领域规范自动生成可执行可验证的终端训练环境；基于该框架构建两个资源：LiteCoder-Terminal-SFT（11,255条专家轨迹，覆盖10个领域）和LiteCoder-Terminal-RL（602个可验证环境用于轨迹级偏好优化）

Result: 在SFT数据集上微调Qwen系列模型，32B变体在Terminal Bench 1.0、2.0和Pro上分别达到29.06%、18.54%和34.00% pass@1；在RL环境上应用直接多轮偏好优化（DMPO）获得额外性能提升

Conclusion: 完全合成的可执行环境为掌握复杂真实世界命令行工作流提供了可扩展且可验证的监督信号

Abstract: Mastering terminal environments requires language agents capable of multi-step planning, feedback-grounded execution, and dynamic state adaptation. However, training such agents is currently bottlenecked by a reliance on scraped external repositories, which limits domain diversity, environment controllability, and the targeting of specific capability deficits. We introduce LiteCoder-Terminal-Gen, a zero-dependency synthesis pipeline that autonomously generates executable and verifiable terminal training environments directly from domain specifications. Using this framework, we construct two large-scale resources: LiteCoder-Terminal-SFT, comprising 11,255 expert trajectories across 10 domains, and LiteCoder-Terminal-RL, featuring 602 verifiable environments for trajectory-level preference optimization. Supervised fine-tuning of Qwen-family models on our SFT dataset yields agents that significantly outperform their base counterparts. Notably, our 32B variant achieves 29.06%, 18.54%, and 34.00% pass@1 on Terminal Bench 1.0, 2.0, and Pro, respectively. Furthermore, applying Direct Multi-turn Preference Optimization (DMPO) on our RL environments yields additional performance gains. These results systematically demonstrate that fully synthetic, executable environments offer a scalable and verifiable supervision signal for mastering complex, real-world command-line workflows.

</details>


### [44] [GAPD: Gold-Action Policy Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning in Knowledge Base Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29584)
*Xin Sun,Jianan Xie,Zhongqi Chen,Qiang Liu,Shu Wu,Bowen Song,Weiqiang Wang,Zilei Wang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GAPD是一种基于强化学习的知识库问答框架，通过金标准动作策略蒸馏为结果导向的RL添加密集的token级指导，使用MID-ANCHOR MATCHING机制对齐学生策略与金标准动作，在WebQSP、GrailQA和GraphQ等基准上超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的KBQA系统主要优化最终答案的稀疏奖励，导致中间动作错误缺乏有效监督，特别是在逻辑形式标注的KBQA基准上，金标准逻辑形式主要用于预热数据构建而非在线策略强化学习更新。

Method: 提出GAPD训练框架，核心采用MID-ANCHOR MATCHING：将学生探索和金标准执行过程中的中间实体视为状态锚点，通过探索的实体集匹配学生状态与金标准状态；将当前策略（基于对齐的金标准动作）作为stop-gradient教师，将其token分布蒸馏回普通学生策略。

Result: GAPD在WebQSP、GrailQA和GraphQ三个基准上持续超越当前最佳性能。

Conclusion: 通过在强化学习训练中引入密集token级指导，有效解决了中间动作错误监督不足的问题，实现了知识库问答任务的性能提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is a natural fit for agentic knowledge base question answering (KBQA), where a model must issue executable actions, observe knowledge-base feedback, and eventually return an answer. However, current RL-based KBQA systems mainly optimize sparse rewards from the final answer, leaving intermediate action errors weakly supervised. This is especially limiting for logical-form annotated KBQA benchmarks: gold logical forms can be converted into executable action sequences, but existing pipelines use them mainly for warm-start data construction rather than for on-policy RL updates. We propose GAPD, a training-time Gold-Action Policy Distillation framework that adds dense token-level guidance to outcome-based RL. To align gold actions with on-policy student rollouts, GAPD uses MID-ANCHOR MATCHING: it treats the intermediate entities reached during student exploration and gold execution as state anchors, and matches student states to gold states through these explored entity sets. The current policy conditioned on this aligned gold action serves as a stop-gradient teacher, whose token distribution is distilled back to the ordinary student policy over generated action-token spans. GAPD consistently surpasses the current state of the art on WebQSP, GrailQA, and GraphQ.

</details>


### [45] [Verifiable Rewards Beyond Math and Code: Lightweight Corpus-Grounded Process Supervision for Factual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29648)
*Shicheng Fan,Haochang Hao,Dehai Min,Weihao Liu,Philip S. Yu,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CorVer提出了一种轻量级、基于维基百科共现统计的句级过程奖励，通过将句子级信用映射到token级优势来提升RL训练中的事实准确性，平均提升TriviaQA 4.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将强化学习应用于知识密集型问答时面临奖励设计困境：响应级奖励仅提供粗粒度监督，无法区分推理轨迹中的正确与错误语句；句级替代方案依赖NLI验证器、LLM裁判或知识验证管道，在RL规模部署昂贵且对罕见实体事实往往不可靠。

Method: CorVer（Corpus Verify）是一种轻量级即插即用的过程奖励，用基于维基百科共现统计的语料库信号替代神经验证器。它为每个句子分配句级信用，并通过简单的对齐将其映射到token级优势，仅需0.5B大小的提取器和每句一次语料库查询。

Result: 在涵盖6个指令微调模型（3B到14B）和5个问答基准的30个（模型、基准）单元格中，CorVer在每个单元格上都优于原始基线，TriviaQA平均提升4.1个百分点。在20个可行配置的单元格中有18个优于四个神经验证器基线，同时训练速度快4.8到8.4倍。

Conclusion: CorVer提供了一种高效、可靠且可扩展的句级奖励设计方案，通过利用公开可用的维基百科语料库统计信息，解决了现有神经验证器在罕见实体事实验证中的不可靠问题，为RL训练中的事实准确性提升提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Applying reinforcement learning to improve factual accuracy in knowledge-intensive question answering faces a reward design dilemma. Response-level rewards provide only coarse supervision and cannot distinguish correct from incorrect statements within a reasoning trace. Sentence-level alternatives offer finer-grained feedback, but typically rely on NLI verifiers, LLM judges, or knowledge-verification pipelines that are expensive to deploy at RL scale and often unreliable for rare-entity facts, where accurate reward signals are especially important. We propose CorVer (Corpus Verify), a lightweight, plug-in-ready process reward that replaces neural verifiers with a corpus-grounded signal derived from Wikipedia co-occurrence statistics. CorVer assigns sentence-level credit and maps it to token-level advantages via a simple alignment, requiring only a 0.5B extractor and a single corpus lookup per sentence. Across 30 (model, benchmark) cells spanning six instruction-tuned models (3B to 14B) and five QA benchmarks, CorVer improves over the raw baseline for every cell, with an average TriviaQA gain of +4.1 pp. It also outperforms four neural-verifier baselines in 18 of 20 cells under their feasible configurations, while training 4.8 to 8.4x faster.

</details>


### [46] [Spurious Prompts: Can Irrelevant Prompts Steer Large Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29678)
*Pawel Batorski,Abtin Pourhadi,Jerzy Sarosiek,Przemyslaw Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究大语言模型对“伪提示词”的敏感性，发现语义与任务无关的提示词也能有效引导模型行为，并提出简单的黑盒搜索方法发现此类提示词


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注任务相关指令、演示或推理提示对大语言模型的影响，尚未深入探究语义上与任务无关的提示词能否引导模型行为

Method: 提出一种简单的黑盒搜索方法，用于发现能够引导模型行为的语义无关提示词

Result: 在推理和问答基准测试中，跨多种模型（0.8B至27B参数，三种模型家族）发现，伪提示词可提升性能，有时优于标准提示和任务感知提示优化；同时能引导模型产生意外行为（如总是选择第一个选项、输出错误答案、无指令地返回偶数、质数或小数）

Conclusion: LLM可以被语义与任务无关的提示词系统性引导，揭示了一种新的提示敏感性形式

Abstract: Large language models are highly sensitive to prompts, but this sensitivity is usually studied through task-relevant instructions, demonstrations, or reasoning cues. In this paper, we study a different form of prompt sensitivity: whether prompts that are semantically unrelated to the task can nevertheless steer model behavior. We call them spurious prompts and show their surprising efficacy. We also propose a simple black-box search procedure for discovering them. Across reasoning and question-answering benchmarks, using models ranging from 0.8B to 27B parameters and spanning three model families, we show that spurious prompts can improve performance, often matching or outperforming standard prompting baselines and task-aware prompt optimization. We further show that they can steer models toward unintended behaviors, such as repeatedly selecting the first answer option, producing incorrect answers, returning an even, prime or small number without explicitly instructing the model to do so. These findings reveal a new kind of prompt sensitivity: LLMs can be systematically steered by prompts that are unrelated to the task they are asked to solve. Our code is available at https://github.com/Batorskq/spurious

</details>


### [47] [Teaching Language Models to Check Grounded Claim Factuality with Human Test-Taking Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29712)
*Yuxuan Ye,Raul Santos-Rodriguez,Edwin Simpson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于阅读理解任务的LLM事实核查方法，通过明确的测试策略减少token使用量超过80%，并训练小型语言模型（SLM）实现低推理成本下的高准确率，在两个事实核查基准测试中达到新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM事实核查方法存在两大问题：1）基于蕴含分类器的指标需要数据集特定阈值调优；2）基于LLM的方法仅使用直接提示，未能充分利用LLM的推理能力。同时，高昂的推理成本限制了这些方法的实际应用。

Method: 1) 将 grounded claim factuality checking 形式化为 true/false 阅读理解任务；2) 使用明确的测试策略提示LLM进行高效推理；3) 训练小型语言模型（SLM）替代LLM，采用监督微调（SFT）和自我修正机制；4) SLM学习生成支持性理由以提高可解释性。

Result: 1) 方法相比无引导开放式推理减少token使用量超过80%；2) 在两个事实核查基准测试中达到竞争力性能，在一个基准上取得新的SOTA；3) 训练后的SLM与强基线性能相当，同时保持低推理成本和可解释性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功平衡了推理效率与性能表现，通过SFT和自我修正机制使小型模型能够在保持准确性的同时生成支持性理由，显著降低了推理成本，同时提升了可解释性，为实际应用提供了更经济有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Grounded claim factuality checking is important for large language model (LLM) applications such as retrieval-augmented generation, as it helps users assess the correctness of generated outputs. Existing metrics using entailment classifiers require dataset-specific threshold tuning, while LLM-based approaches often use direct prompting, which underutilises the reasoning capabilities of LLMs. We address this by formulating grounded claim factuality checking as a true/false reading comprehension task and prompting LLMs with explicit test-taking strategies for efficient reasoning. Our method reduces token usage by over 80% compared to unguided open-ended reasoning, and achieves competitive performance to more expensive alternatives across two factuality benchmarks, setting a new state of the art on one. To further reduce inference cost, we train small language models (SLMs) to replace LLMs in the checking pipeline. Using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and a self-revision mechanism, the SLMs learn to improve their factuality judgements. Experimental results show that the resulting SLMs perform on par with strong baselines, combining low inference costs with generating supporting rationales to support interpretability. Code and datasets will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [48] [User-Aware Active Knowledge Acquisition for Emotional Support Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29715)
*Mufan Xu,Kehai Chen,Jiahao Hu,Xinchao Xu,Muyun Yang,Tiejun Zhao,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UKA（User-Aware Active Knowledge Acquisition）是一种梯度无关的主动对话学习框架，通过引入主动学习和心智理论不确定性估计机制，有效提升情感支持对话系统对用户隐式需求的理解和响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感支持对话系统需要适应多轮交互中用户的演变和隐式需求，但由于用户需求信号通常微弱、间接，且需要通过多轮交互才能明确，现有方法难以高效获取和泛化相关的对话知识。

Method: UKA框架采用梯度无关的主动对话学习策略，显式表示对用户需求的不确定性，并将主动学习应用于知识获取和响应选择两个环节。该方法提出了一种基于心智理论的（Theory-of-Mind）不确定性估计机制，使模型能够优先选择响应，从而激发更多信息的用户反馈。

Result: 在多个对话基准和模型架构上的实验表明，UKA在对话质量和用户对齐方面持续优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: UKA通过在训练期间高效探索用户对齐的对话知识，同时保持测试时的鲁棒性，成功解决了情感支持对话系统中知识获取不足的问题，为自适应情感支持提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Emotional support plays an important role in dialogue systems, and its success depends on adapting to a user's evolving and implicit needs across multi-turn interactions while leveraging the strong reasoning capacity of large language models. However, since signals about user needs are often weak, indirect, and can only be disambiguated through multi-turn interaction, existing emotional support methods often struggle to acquire and generalize relevant conversational knowledge efficiently. To bridge this gap, we introduce User-Aware Active Knowledge Acquisition (UKA), a gradient-free active dialogue learning framework that explicitly represents uncertainty about user needs and incorporates active learning into both knowledge acquisition and response selection.We propose a Theory-of-Mind uncertainty estimation mechanism that allows the model to prioritize responses, thereby eliciting more informative user feedback. UKA is capable of efficiently exploring user-aligned conversational knowledge during training while maintaining robustness at test time. Experiments across multiple dialogue benchmarks and model architectures demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms strong baselines in dialogue quality and user alignment.

</details>


### [49] [HTAM: Hierarchical Transition-Attended Memory for Operator Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29734)
*Yining Zhang,Mingyang Yi,Chen Wang,Xuwen Xiang,Tianhe Jia,Zedong Dan,Chengqing Zong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HTAM是一种用于LLM驱动的GPU算子优化的粗粒度到细粒度分层框架，通过两级层次化转移图组织优化经验，解决现有方法中粒度不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化仍需专业知识，现有LLM方法面临粒度不匹配：粗粒度提示可复用但难以执行，细粒度记忆可操作但扩大搜索空间并掩盖优化瓶颈。核心挑战是如何在适当粒度组织优化经验。

Method: 提出HTAM框架，构建两级层次化转移图（HTG）来组织粗粒度全局方向、细粒度局部策略以及步骤间的转移经验。在每次演化步骤中，HTAM从当前状态和近期优化历史中选择全局方向，检索相应的局部策略记忆，并用于指导CUDA代码生成。

Result: 在KernelBench套件上的实验显示HTAM在正确性、快速解决方案率和加速比方面持续优于LLM基线。后端和Robust-KBench研究表明结构化记忆具有可迁移的益处。

Conclusion: HTAM通过在适当粒度组织优化经验有效解决了LLM驱动GPU算子优化中的粒度不匹配问题，在多个评估指标上取得一致改进，并展现出结构化记忆的可迁移优势。

Abstract: High-performance GPU kernels are essential for efficient LLM deployment, yet optimizing them remains expertise-intensive. Recent LLM-based code generation makes automatic GPU operator generation promising, but operator optimization remains a hardware-aware search problem. Existing LLM-based methods face a granularity mismatch: coarse hints are reusable but hard to execute, whereas detailed memories are actionable but enlarge the search space and obscure optimization bottlenecks. The key challenge is therefore to organize optimization experience at an appropriate granularity. To address this issue, this paper proposes HTAM (Hierarchical Transition-Attended Memory), a coarse-to-fine framework for LLM-based operator optimization. HTAM builds a two-level Hierarchical Transition Graph (HTG) to organize coarse global directions, detailed local strategies, and transition experience between optimization steps. During each evolution step, HTAM selects a global direction from the current state and recent optimization history, retrieves the corresponding local strategy memory, and uses it to guide concrete CUDA code generation. Experiments on the full KernelBench suite demonstrate that HTAM consistently improves correctness, fast-solution rate, and speedup over LLM-based baselines, while backend and Robust-KBench studies indicate transferable benefits from structured memory.

</details>


### [50] [Multi-Legal-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Legal Reasoning Across Jurisdictions, Languages, and Legal Traditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29738)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个跨司法管辖区法律基准Multi-Legal-Bench，涵盖6个国家、4种语言家族和1.34亿份法院判决书，定义了5项任务，评估了7个前沿大模型和4个小/中型模型，发现跨语言迁移效果与语言接近度无关，标签集对齐比语言家族更能预测迁移质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律NLP基准大多仅评估单一语言或汇总任务，而不同司法管辖区的任务本质差异使得跨语言比较变得不可能。

Method: 引入Multi-Legal-Bench基准，在6个国家（乌克兰、法国、荷兰、波兰、捷克、立陶宛）、4种语言家族、1.34亿份法院判决书上定义5项任务（法院类型分类、判决形式分类、案件结果预测、法律规范提取、原因类别预测），形成5×6任务-司法管辖区矩阵（20/30单元格填充）。评估7个前沿LLM（通过AWS Bedrock进行零样本和3样本提示）以及4个额外的小/中型模型（3-12B参数量）。

Result: （1）在乌克兰发现的任务依赖性少样本效应在所有司法管辖区都重复出现；（2）没有单一模型在任何语言排名中占据主导地位，排名随任务和司法管辖区变化；（3）跨语言少样本迁移不遵循语言接近度：UA→FR（罗曼语族，-2.1个百分点）比UA→PL（斯拉夫语族，-13.7个百分点）效果更好，标签集对齐比语言家族更能预测迁移质量；（4）尽管tokenizer多样性存在2.3倍差距，但未显著预测跨语言准确率（r=-0.27，p=0.14）。

Conclusion: 模型架构和预训练数据主导tokenizer效率；标签集对齐优于语言家族作为跨语言迁移质量预测指标；法律NLP跨司法管辖区评估需要统一任务定义。

Abstract: Legal NLP benchmarks overwhelmingly evaluate a single language or aggregate tasks that differ fundamentally across jurisdictions, making cross-lingual comparison impossible. We introduce Multi-Legal-Bench, the first cross-jurisdictional legal benchmark that evaluates identical tasks across six countries (Ukraine, France, Netherlands, Poland, Czech Republic, Lithuania), four language families, and 134 million court decisions. The benchmark defines five tasks court-type classification, judgment form classification, case-outcome prediction, legal norm extraction, and cause category prediction mapped to structured metadata from national court registries, forming a deliberately sparse 5x6 task-jurisdiction matrix (20 of 30 cells filled). We evaluate 7 frontier LLMs under zero-shot and 3-shot prompting via AWS Bedrock, with 4 additional small/medium models (3-12B) for scaling analysis. Our results reveal that: (1) task-dependent few-shot effects discovered in Ukrainian replicate across all jurisdictions; (2) no single model dominates any language rankings shift with both task and jurisdiction; (3) cross-lingual few-shot transfer does not follow language proximity: UA->FR (Romance, -2.1 pp) transfers better than UA->PL (Slavic, -13.7 pp), with label-set alignment predicting transfer quality better than language family; and (4) tokenizer fertility, despite a 2.3x spread, does not significantly predict cross-lingual accuracy (r=-0.27, p=0.14), suggesting that model architecture and pretraining data dominate tokenizer efficiency. We release all data, prompts, and model predictions.

</details>


### [51] [ActTraitBench: Quantifying the Knowledge-Decision Gap in Large Language Models via Human-Grounded Behavioral Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29791)
*Yutong Yang,Chenxi Miao,Weikang Li,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ActTraitBench框架用于评估LLM人格一致性，发现知识-决策差距并引入CoCA方法缓解对齐问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽能 convincingly 模拟人格，但在隐性行为决策中常偏离，现有基准测试因构念效度有限、多维度纠缠和分布偏差难以测量这种不对称性

Method: ActTraitBench框架：基于人类数据，建立心理测量方面与行为范式的一一对应，通过分位数映射（Distributional Calibration via Quantile Mapping）对齐LLM评判者分数分布与人类规范；引入Chain of Cognitive Alignment（CoCA）推理时干预方法

Result: 14个主流LLM实验发现普遍存在知识-决策不对称：更大型和更强大的模型尽管自我报告高度一致，行为偏差反而更强；CoCA提升推理型前沿模型对齐，暴露小型架构能力局限

Conclusion: LLM中存在显著知识-决策差距，大模型行为一致性不随能力提升而改善；CoCA为提升对齐提供解决方案，凸显不同架构模型能力差异

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) can convincingly simulate personas in explicit self-reports, they often deviate in implicit behavioral decisions, revealing a substantial Knowledge-Decision Gap ($G_{\text{KD}}$). Existing benchmarks struggle to measure this asymmetry due to limited construct validity, multi-dimensional entanglement, and distributional biases in LLM-based evaluation. To address these issues, we propose ActTraitBench, a human-grounded evaluation framework for measuring personality consistency in LLMs. Grounded in empirical human data, ActTraitBench establishes one-to-one mappings between psychometric facets and behavioral paradigms, and applies a Distributional Calibration via Quantile Mapping procedure to align LLM-judge score distributions with human norms. Experiments on 14 mainstream LLMs reveal a pervasive knowledge-decision asymmetry, where larger and more capable models often exhibit stronger behavioral divergence despite highly consistent self-reports. To mitigate this gap, we further introduce the Chain of Cognitive Alignment (CoCA), a plug-and-play inference-time intervention that improves alignment in reasoning-capable frontier models while exposing clear capability limitations in smaller architectures.

</details>


### [52] [Nine Judges, Two Effective Votes: Correlated Errors Undermine LLM Evaluation Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29800)
*Guneet Kohli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过测量9个前沿大语言模型组成的评估面板，发现它们实际上只提供了相当于2个独立投票的信息量，约75%的名义独立性因模型在同一项目上犯相同的错误而丢失。结论是评估瓶颈在于评估者之间的相关性而非聚合算法，单纯增加评估者数量无法替代真正独立的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge 评估框架通过多个模型的投票聚合来提高评估可靠性，但假设不同模型之间具有足够的独立性。然而，模型可能在同一问题上犯相同错误，导致投票冗余。本文旨在量化这种冗余程度，评估实际可靠性偏离独立投票理想情况有多远，以及如何改进评估框架。

Method: 开发了一个框架来测量评估面板的真实信息价值和可靠性缺口。在三个自然语言推理数据集上测试了来自7个模型家族的9个前沿LLM，每个项目有100个人类标注。使用Kish有效样本量和Condorcet空模型来量化发现，并测试了各种提示变体、温度、思维链推理和成对偏好任务。

Result: 9个评估者实际上只提供了约2个独立投票的信息量，约75%的名义独立性因模型在同一项目上犯相同的错误而丢失。评估面板的实际准确率比独立投票低8-22个百分点，最佳单个评估者与完整面板的表现相当或更好。即使使用正确的答案和更聪明的聚合算法，现有方法最多只能缩小11%的差距。

Conclusion: 评估瓶颈在于评估者之间的相关性而非聚合算法。单纯增加评估者数量无法替代真正独立的评估。这意味着LLM-as-a-judge 框架需要从根本上解决模型之间的相关性问题，而不仅仅是优化聚合策略。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge panels aggregate votes from multiple models, with the expectation that diverse models yield more reliable evaluations. We develop a framework to measure the true informational value of such panels and quantify how far their reliability falls short of the independent-voting ideal. Testing a panel of 9 frontier LLMs from 7 model families on three natural language inference datasets (each with 100 human annotations per item), we find that the 9 judges effectively provide only about 2 independent votes' worth of information. Roughly three-quarters of the panel's nominal independence is lost because the models make the same mistakes on the same items. The consequences are stark: the panel's actual accuracy falls 8-22 percentage points short of what independent voting would achieve, and the best single judge matches or outperforms the full panel across all conditions. Neither adding more judges nor using smarter aggregation algorithms helps -- established methods close at most 11% of this gap, even with access to the correct answers. We quantify these findings using the Kish effective sample size (n_eff) and a Condorcet null model, and show the deficit is robust across prompt variants, temperatures, chain-of-thought reasoning, and a pairwise preference task (RewardBench). The bottleneck is correlated judges, not the aggregation algorithm, implying that scaling up panels cannot substitute for genuinely independent evaluation.

</details>


### [53] [Towards Localized and Disentangled Knowledge Editing for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29826)
*Leijiang Gu,Zhen Zeng,Feng Li,Xinjian Gao,Zenglin Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LDKE通过快速定位关键层和解纠缠分类器，解决了多模态知识编辑中因果错位和特征纠缠导致的泛化失败及无关信息意外修改问题，实现了精确且通用的编辑效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MKE方法仅能有效修改目标事实对，但无法将编辑泛化到逻辑相关的查询，且常导致无关但视觉或语义关联信息的意外修改。研究识别出两种根本失效模式：Causal Misalignment（将编辑限制在特定样本）和Feature Entanglement（导致耦合但无关信息的意外修改）。

Method: LDKE框架包含两个核心模块：1) Fast Localization模块，高效识别和更新关键层；2) Disentanglement Classifier，适当路由输入以保留无关知识。该框架通过定位特定事实的模型层并将目标相关输入与无关输入解纠缠，实现精确且通用的编辑。

Result: 在多个基准测试和MLLM上的广泛实验表明，LDKE在向相关上下文传播编辑方面表现出色，同时保持了高局部性，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: LDKE成功解决了MKE中的因果错位和特征纠缠问题，通过精确的层定位和输入解纠缠，实现了既能泛化到相关查询又能保持无关信息完整性的理想编辑效果。

Abstract: Existing methods in Multimodal Knowledge Editing (MKE) have advanced the ability to correct outdated or inaccurate knowledge in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, they exhibit a critical limitation: while effectively modifying target factual pairs, they fail to generalize edits to logically related queries and often cause unintended alterations to unrelated but visually or semantically linked information. We identify and formalize two underlying failure modes causing this issue: Causal Misalignment, which confines edits to the specific sample, and Feature Entanglement, which causes unintended alterations to coupled but irrelevant information. To address these issues, we propose Localized and Disentangled Knowledge Editing (LDKE), a new framework that achieves precise and generalized editing by localizing fact-specific model layers and disentangling target-relevant inputs from irrelevant ones. Our approach introduces a Fast Localization module to identify and update critical layers efficiently, along with a Disentanglement Classifier that routes inputs appropriately to preserve unrelated knowledge. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks and MLLMs demonstrate that LDKE achieves superior performance in propagating edits to related contexts while maintaining high locality.

</details>


### [54] [EvoRubric: Self-Evolving Rubric-Driven RL for Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29847)
*Xin Guan,Xiaomeng Hu,Shen Huang,Zhenyi Wang,Bo Zhang,Zijian Li,Pengjun Xie,Bo Liu,Jiuxin Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EvoRubric是一个单策略协同进化RL框架，通过统一响应生成和标准生成来动态交替推理者和标准生成器，解决了开放式生成任务中奖励缺失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在LLM对齐中的主要挑战是开放式生成任务缺乏明确的奖励信号。现有的基于标准的方法依赖静态、人工标注的标准，导致策略滞后或需要昂贵的外部专有模型。

Method: EvoRubric提出一个单参数化策略框架，统一响应生成和标准生成。它动态交替推理者和标准生成器，引入多级验证管道（包括元验证器、零方差剪枝和留一法同行共识机制），动态归档有效标准到内存池。

Result: 在医疗、写作和科学领域的实验中，EvoRubric显著优于传统静态和外部LLM驱动的对齐方法。框架兼容人类专家先验，初始化专家标注标准可发现新的判别维度。

Conclusion: 该方法通过协同进化机制克服了开放式生成任务奖励挑战，在多领域展现出显著优势，实现了比单纯依赖静态专家标注更好的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has significantly advanced Large Language Models (LLMs) in verifiable domains, but aligning models for open-ended generation remains profoundly challenging due to the lack of definitive rewards. Current rubric-based RL methods mitigate this by employing explicit criteria; however, they rely heavily on static, human-annotated rubrics that inevitably cause policy lag, or expensive external proprietary models for dynamic updates. In this paper, we propose EvoRubric, a novel single-policy co-evolutionary RL framework that eliminates the reliance on static criteria and on external rubric generators. By unifying response generation and rubric generation under a single parameterized policy, EvoRubric dynamically alternates between a Reasoner and a Rubric Generator. To prevent reward hacking and ensure the reliability of generated signals, we introduce a multi-level verification pipeline featuring a meta-verifier, zero-variance pruning, and a Leave-One-Out peer consensus mechanism. Validated criteria are dynamically archived into a memory pool, yielding dense, multi-objective rewards to continuously co-optimize both roles. Extensive experiments across Medical, Writing, and Science domains demonstrate that EvoRubric consistently outperforms traditional static and external-LLM-driven alignment methods. Notably, our framework is compatible with human-expert priors. When initialized with expert-annotated rubrics, EvoRubric can further uncover novel, discriminative dimensions, achieving better performance than relying solely on static expert annotations.

</details>


### [55] [Towards Verifiable Multimodal Deep Research: A Multi-Agent Harness for Interleaved Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29861)
*Chenghao Zhang,Guanting Dong,Yufan Liu,Tong Zhao,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Ptah多智能体框架，用于生成可验证的跨模态深度研究报告，通过规划、研究和写作阶段协调从用户查询到报告渲染的完整流程，并引入PtahEval评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs已从深度搜索（获取简明事实答案）发展到深度研究（将分散证据综合成长篇报告），但可验证的多模态深度研究仍面临挑战：缺乏确定性真实参考、需要在文本论证与视觉证据间交替呈现。

Method: Ptah是一个多智能体编排框架，包含规划、研究和写作三个阶段：专用智能体构建视觉感知计划、收集基于主张的证据、在“视觉工作记忆”中维护源对齐图像，并使用声明式多模态工具组合报告；验证智能体作为框架的接受函数，确保事实依据、引用准确性和跨模态一致性。

Result: 在深度研究基准测试中，Ptah相比强基线生成更可靠、视觉信息更丰富且更易用的人类可读多模态报告。

Conclusion: Ptah成功解决了多模态深度研究中的开放性综合与跨模态证据呈现问题，并通过PtahEval提供了细粒度的图像级和展示级评估，显著提升了多模态报告的质量与可验证性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced autonomous agents from deep search, which retrieves concise factual answers, to deep research, which synthesizes scattered evidence into long-form reports. However, verifiable multimodal deep research remains challenging due to open-ended synthesis without deterministic ground truth and the need to interleave textual arguments with visual evidence. We propose \textsc{Ptah}, a multi-agent harness for interleaved report generation. \textsc{Ptah} orchestrates the lifecycle from user query to rendered web report through planning, research, and writing stages, where specialized agents construct visual-aware plans, collect claim-grounded evidence, maintain source-aligned images in a \textit{Visual Working Memory}, and compose reports through declarative multimodal tool use. A verifier agent serves as the harness's acceptance function, enforcing factual grounding, citation fidelity, and cross-modal consistency throughout the workflow. We further introduce \textsc{Ptah}Eval, an evaluation protocol that augments existing benchmarks with image-level and presentation-level assessments. Experiments on deep research benchmarks show that \textsc{Ptah} produces more reliable, visually informative, and usable human-facing multimodal reports than strong baselines.

</details>


### [56] [ExCAM: Explainable Cultural Awareness Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29897)
*Christoph Leiter,Haiyue Song,Hour Kaing,Jin Tei,Hideki Tanaka,Masao Utiyama,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ExCAM，首个专注于识别、评分和解释指令-输出对中文化错误的解释性文化感知指标，通过ExCAM40k数据集（整合9个基准并添加合成错误）实现最高80%的检测准确率，为自由文本的文化评估提供细粒度且可解释的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型的文化感知能力对确保生成文本的公平性和应用的全球泛化性至关重要。现有基准测试虽能评估文化商品（如食物）和文化价值观（如压力情境下的行为），但存在三个主要问题：1）创建基准需要耗时的昂贵人工标注；2）评估自由文本文化感知的基准稀缺；3）现有评估机制往往依赖过时的方法。

Method: 提出ExCAM（Explainable Cultural Awareness Metric），首个专门用于识别、评分和解释指令-输出对中文化错误的指标。配套引入ExCAM40k数据集，将9个现有基准重新格式化并增强合成错误。ExCAM通过分析模型输出与指令之间的文化不匹配，提供错误定位、评分和解释性分析。

Result: 在平衡测试集上，ExCAM相比包括GPT-5在内的多个基线实现了最高的错误检测率，准确率达到80%。这证明了ExCAM在文化感知评估方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ExCAM为自由文本的文化评估开辟了新路径，实现了细粒度且可解释的文化感知评估，有助于推动跨文化应用的发展。

Abstract: Evaluating the cultural awareness of large language models is crucial to ensure the fairness of generated text and the generalizability of applications across the world. Recent benchmarks explore cultural goods like food or values like behavior in stressful situations through the lens of question answering or text generation tasks. However, creating these benchmarks requires time-intensive and costly human annotations. Also, benchmarks that evaluate cultural awareness in free text are scarce and often rely on dated evaluation mechanisms. To address this gap, we introduce ExCAM, an Explainable Cultural Awareness Metric, which is, to our knowledge, the first dedicated evaluation metric that identifies, rates and explains cultural errors in instruction-output pairs. To train and evaluate ExCAM, we introduce ExCAM40k, a dataset comprised of nine existing benchmarks that we reformat and enhance with synthetic errors. Compared to several baselines, including GPT-5, ExCAM achieves the highest error detection rate with up to 80% accuracy on a balanced test set. Therefore, ExCAM opens the pathway towards fine-grained and explainable cultural evaluation of free text.

</details>


### [57] [Latent Performance Profiling of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30018)
*Tanmoy Chakraborty,Ayan Sengupta,Suparna Bhattacharya,Partha Pratim Chakrabarti,Amlan Chakrabarti,Supratik Chakraborty,Partha Pratim Das,Lipika Dey,Richa Singh,Mayank Vatsa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型内部隐状态和输出分布的Latent Performance Profiling（LPP）评估框架，旨在超越基准测试的表面准确率，提供更深层的模型能力理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在标准化基准测试中表现优异，但单纯准确率无法全面反映其能力。基于排行榜的评估面临数据污染、任务范围狭窄、与现实可靠性脱节等问题。基准测试主要关注模型输出的"什么"，而非"如何"处理信息、校准不确定性或构建知识结构。

Method: 提出Latent Performance Profiling（LPP）框架，从隐藏激活和输出分布中提取任务无关的诊断指标。通过定义模型潜在表示和动态的标量度量，揭示尺度无关的特征，用于可解释的比较和发现隐藏漏洞。与静态准确率不同，LPP在相似规模模型间提供稳定且架构敏感的签名。

Result: 在8个LLM（0.5B-14B参数量）上进行广泛实证分析，发现基准分数相似的模型表现出截然不同的潜在特征，如熵或适应性的差异。设计的合成探针用于不确定性和符号推理，与内在指标对齐同时与排行榜偏见解耦。

Conclusion: LPP在相似规模模型间提供稳定的架构敏感签名。建议将LPP与基准测试一起报告，提供更深层的可解释模型行为理解，实现更可靠的模型选择、安全评估和超越表面准确率的评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently achieve impressive scores on standardized benchmarks, yet accuracy alone offers a limited view of their capabilities. Evaluating open-source LLMs through leaderboards faces persistent issues like data contamination, narrow task scope, and weak alignment with real-world reliability. Benchmark-based evaluations such as MMLU PRO, BBH, or IFEval primarily capture \textit{what} a model outputs on fixed test sets, not \textit{how} it processes information, calibrates uncertainty, or structures internal knowledge. In this article, we advocate for a shift from benchmark-centric evaluation toward a complementary, \textit{state-centered intrinsic assessment} of LLMs. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Latent Performance Profiling (LPP)} -- a framework that derives task-agnostic diagnostics from hidden activations and output distributions. LPP defines a set of scalar metrics on a model's latent representations and dynamics, revealing scale-independent traits that enable interpretable comparisons and uncover hidden vulnerabilities. Unlike static accuracy scores, LPP provides stable, architecture-sensitive signatures across models of similar size. With extensive empirical analyses across eight LLMs, spanning a size range of 0.5B-14B, we demonstrate that models with similar benchmark scores can exhibit contrasting latent profiles, such as differences in entropy or adaptability. Guided by these insights, we design synthetic probes for uncertainty and symbolic reasoning that align with intrinsic metrics while decoupling from leaderboard bias. We recommend that reporting LPP alongside benchmarks provides a deeper, interpretable understanding of model behavior, enabling more reliable model selection, safety assessment, and evaluation beyond surface-level accuracy.

</details>


### [58] [Who Am I? History-Aware Profiles for Student Simulation in Tutoring Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30051)
*Zhangqi Duan,Shuyan Huang,Alexander Scarlatos,Jaewook Lee,Simon Woodhead,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于学习历史的条件化学生模拟任务，通过构建包含学生画像生成器和对话模拟器的双组件框架，利用强化学习优化学生模拟的忠实度，在真实数学学习平台数据集上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自动辅导工具中的学生模拟工作主要关注单次对话内的模拟，缺乏对学生知识水平和行为的历史上下文理解。这是因为现有方法未基于学生过往的问答或对话交互进行建模。为了更好地评估和训练辅导模型，需要一种能够利用学生完整学习历史的模拟方法。

Method: 提出历史条件化学生模拟任务，核心是利用学生学习历史来准确预测学生对话轮次。采用双组件框架：(1)画像生成器：总结学生历史信息，生成学生画像；(2)模拟器：基于画像条件预测学生对话轮次。两个组件均使用强化学习(RL)进行训练，以优化画像的忠实度。

Result: 在首个真实世界数学学习平台的学生对话和问答数据集上评估。实验表明，所提方法在多个指标上显著优于基线方法。同时证明了学习历史、学生画像以及强化学习训练对于实现忠实的学生模拟至关重要。

Conclusion: 历史条件化学生模拟是LLM驱动的自动辅导工具开发中的关键任务。通过结合历史信息、画像建模和强化学习训练，可以显著提升学生模拟的准确性，为辅导模型的有效评估和训练提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: A key part of developing large language model (LLM)-powered, automated tutoring tools is student simulation, i.e., using LLMs to role-play as students, which can facilitate tutor model evaluation and training. Existing work mostly focuses on within-dialogue simulation, which lacks context on student knowledge and behavior, partly due to not grounding in past student question-answering or dialogue interactions. In this work, we introduce the task of history-conditioned student simulation, where the goal is to accurately predict student dialogue turns by leveraging information in the student's learning history. We propose a two-component framework in which a profile generator summarizes a student's history and a simulator predicts student turns conditioned on the resulting profile. We train both components with reinforcement learning (RL), yielding profiles optimized for faithful student simulation. We evaluate our method and baselines on the first-of-its-kind real-world dataset of student dialogues and question responses that we collect from a math learning platform. Extensive experiments show that our method significantly outperforms baselines, and demonstrate the importance of history, profiles, and RL training.

</details>

Abstract: While LLM agents have demonstrated remarkable task-oriented abilities such as planning, reasoning, and action, few works have treated them as complete human personalities where emotional dimensions hold equal importance. In this paper, we introduce a novel benchmark to systematically assess whether LLM agents can simulate coherent, human-like psychology. Specifically, our benchmark constructs 11 diverse human characters grounded in orthogonal Big Five personality traits, with each profile deeply integrated with 1,000 structured autobiographical-style episodic memories distributed across theory-grounded developmental life stages. To rigorously evaluate the psychological manifestations of LLMs, we designed a curated suite of 64 decision-making scenarios, guided by the DIAMONDS taxonomy, a psychological framework that characterizes situations along eight dimensions: Duty, Intellect, Adversity, Mating, pOsitivity, Negativity, Deception, and Sociality. By subjecting agents to varying scenarios, the benchmark evaluates whether they can consolidate their innate personality traits and autobiographical memories to make behavioral decisions that are consistent with their specific psychological profiles. After systematic human validation and filtering, we obtained a benchmark consisting of 673 multiple-choice questions (MCQs). We believe this benchmark provides a principled and scalable testbed for studying human-like emotions, personality consistency, and value-consistent behavioural decision-making in LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [60] [UniSteer: Text-Guided Flow Matching in Activation Space for Versatile LLM Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30076)
*Yingdong Shi,Ruiming Zhang,Changming Li,Zhiyu Yang,Kaixing Zhang,Jingyi Yu,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniSteer是一种文本引导的激活流匹配模型，通过学习条件速度场而非为每种行为单独干预，实现对LLM行为、真实性、细粒度概念、多约束指令和激活空间分类的统一控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活控制方法依赖固定的引导方向或特定任务干预模块，难以适应细粒度概念和组合约束，需要一种更灵活、可泛化的统一控制接口。

Method: UniSteer学习基于自然语言条件的残差流激活的条件分布（通用条件速度场），推理时通过流逆变换：部分迁移源激活到潜在状态→在目标文本条件下重新生成→注入冻结LLM；同时支持激活空间分类（选择重建能量最低的文本标签）。

Result: 在三个目标LLM上的实验表明，UniSteer为行为控制、真实性引导、细粒度概念引导、多约束指令遵循和激活空间分类提供了统一的接口，表现出良好的泛化性和适应性。

Conclusion: UniSteer通过流匹配和条件速度场学习，实现了对LLM激活空间的灵活、细粒度、多约束控制，且无需为每种行为单独训练干预模块，展现了强大的通用性和跨任务适用性。

Abstract: Activation-based control steers large language models (LLMs) by intervening on their internal representations during inference, and has emerged as an effective paradigm for controlling behaviors such as persona and style. However, existing methods often rely on fixed steering directions or task-specific intervention modules, making them difficult to adapt to fine-grained concepts and compositional constraints. We propose UniSteer, a text-guided activation flow matching model that learns a conditional distribution over residual-stream activations from natural-language conditions. Instead of fitting a separate intervention for each target behavior, UniSteer learns a universal conditional velocity field in activation space. At inference time, UniSteer performs flow inversion by partially transporting a source activation toward a latent state and regenerating it under a target textual condition before injecting it back into the frozen LLM. The same conditional model supports activation-space classification by selecting the textual label with the lowest reconstruction energy. Experiments on three target LLMs show that UniSteer provides a unified interface across behavioral control, truthfulness steering, fine-grained concept steering, multi-constraint instruction following, and activation-space classification.

</details>

Abstract: Radiology report generation (RRG) is commonly formulated as a single-path generation task, where a multimodal large language model (MLLM) produces one decoded report as the final output. While recent progress has largely been driven by scaling training data, model capacity, and retrieval mechanisms, improving report quality at inference time remains underexplored. In this work, we observe that fixed radiology MLLMs often generate clinically stronger reports elsewhere in their candidate pool than the one selected by default decoding, suggesting that inference-time decision making remains an overlooked bottleneck. To address this, we propose Clinical Consensus Selection (CCS), a decoder-agnostic inference-time selection framework that samples multiple candidate reports and selects the one with the highest clinical consensus across the rollout pool. CCS unifies text-based utilities with a radiology-adapted utility computed by an image--report-trained multimodal embedder, which measures candidate agreement beyond surface-level textual similarity. Across three datasets and multiple radiology MLLMs, CCS consistently improves inference-time performance over single-path decoding and generic Best-of-N baselines, with particularly clear gains on clinical metrics. Further analysis shows that image-grounded utility forms a selection axis distinct from textual consensus and that substantial headroom remains for improving RRG at inference time.

</details>

Abstract: Third-person singular pronouns have long been used to study stereotypical biases in language models and to test their abilities to reason about reference. More recently, the interplay between reasoning and bias has been investigated with the task of pronoun fidelity, which assesses models' abilities to correctly reuse a previously-specified pronoun for a discourse entity, independent of other potentially distracting discourse entities mentioned in between. However, such research focuses on English, which is a language with limited grammatical gender and almost no gender agreement. In this paper we contribute a novel, large-scale dataset, GRUFF, to measure pronoun fidelity in German, covering four different gender agreement systems in nouns, and four sets of pronouns. With this dataset, we show that LLMs show strong grammatical agreement for masculine and feminine entities in the absence of explicit context, but not for neopronouns xier and en. Models are generally not robust to distractors, but encoder-only models are more robust in German than in English, reflecting the importance of grammatical gender. Finally, we show that occupational stereotypes in this context are poorly correlated across grammatical cases, and across most models, except ones with closely related architectures. We release all code and data to encourage further work on gender-inclusive language and referential reasoning in German.

</details>


### [63] [Knowing What to Solve Before How: Preplan Empowered LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30245)
*Shaojie Wang,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PPC框架通过引入明确的"预计划"阶段，将推理范式从question→plan→CoT扩展为question→preplan→plan→CoT，在数学推理任务上取得显著提升，且不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计划推理方法（question→plan→CoT）存在隐式问题理解阶段，未明确识别问题类型、适用工具和潜在陷阱，导致计划与执行脱节，形成范式级缺口。

Method: 提出PPC框架，引入"预计划"阶段实现question→preplan→plan→CoT范式。采用三阶段合成管道+spoiler-score检测器构建干净预计划监督，使用复合GRPO奖励确保计划真正遵循预计划。

Result: 在4个模型和5个数学推理基准上，PPC在40个指标中39个达到最优，maj@16和pass@16分别提升2.23和3.06，且不增加推理token开销。

Conclusion: PPC通过显式问题理解阶段有效弥合了计划与执行间的缺口，在数学推理任务上表现优异，验证了问题理解在推理过程中的关键作用。

Abstract: Current plan-based reasoning methods improve large language models (LLMs) by inserting a planning stage before execution, giving rise to the question $\rightarrow$ plan $\rightarrow$ cot paradigm. While effective, a closer examination reveals an inherent paradigm-level gap: both the planning and its execution stages decide how to solve a problem, while the prior question of what to solve; recognizing the problem type, the applicable tools, and the foreseeable pitfalls; remains entirely implicit. To bridge this gap, we propose PPC (Preplan-Plan-CoT), a framework that introduces an explicit problem-understanding stage, the preplan, yielding a new question $\rightarrow$ preplan $\rightarrow$ plan $\rightarrow$ cot paradigm. Realizing this paradigm requires safeguarding the conceptual integrity of preplan at both ends. Specifically, we design a three-stage synthesis pipeline with a spoiler-score detector that filters out leakage and spoiler failures to build clean preplan supervision, and a composite GRPO reward enforces that the generated plan genuinely follows from the preplan. Experiments across four backbones and five mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that PPC achieves the best results on 39 of 40 metrics, improving maj@16 and pass@16 by +2.23 and +3.06 over the strongest baseline without introducing additional inference token overhead.

</details>


### [64] [Same Evidence, Different Answers: Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30251)
*Zizhuo Lin,Quanling Liu,Jinsheng Quan,Chao Zhang,Yifan Zhu,Xing Shi,Jingtao Xu,Zhihui Li,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation (CCOPD)方法，通过训练使LLM在逐步揭示信息时对齐自身行为与完整上下文行为，解决RAW-SHARDED场景下的性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在单次提示中能很好完成任务，但当信息分多轮逐步揭示时性能会下降。即使相同信息以完整或分散方式呈现，模型应给出相同答案，但实际存在差距。作者认为原因是"自我锚定漂移"：部分信息下产生的响应引入未支持的假设，这些假设扭曲最终答案。

Method: 提出Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation (CCOPD)。训练时同一基础模型扮演两个角色：被冻结的教师模型（条件是清洁完整提示）和可训练的学生模型（接收逐步揭示证据）。CCOPD对齐学生在自己轨迹上的行为与教师的规范全上下文行为。

Result: 仅在数学问题对话上训练的CCOPD在RAW-SHARDED性能上比原始基础模型平均提高32%，涵盖数学和五个零样本跨域任务家族，同时基本保持全上下文性能。进一步分析表明CCOPD加强用户证据锚定，减少对先前助手轮次污染的敏感性。

Conclusion: CCOPD有效缓解LLMs在逐步信息揭示下的性能下降问题，通过训练使模型对齐部分上下文行为与全上下文行为，提高RAW-SHARDED场景表现，同时保持全上下文性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often solve a task when all instructions are given in a single prompt, but fail when the same information is revealed gradually across turns. When a clean FULL prompt and a RAW-SHARDED conversation contain the same complete user evidence, the model should still arrive at the same answer. We argue that a key reason for this gap is self-anchored drift: responses produced under partial information introduce unsupported assumptions, and those assumptions later distort the final answer. To reduce this effect, we propose Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation (CCOPD). During training, the same base model is used in two roles: a frozen teacher conditioned on the clean FULL prompt and a trainable student that receives the same evidence incrementally through a multi-turn conversation; CCOPD aligns the student's behavior on its own trajectories with the teacher's canonical full-context behavior. Trained only on math problem conversations, CCOPD yields a 32\% average relative improvement in RAW-SHARDED performance over the original base model across math and five zero-shot out-of-domain task families, while largely preserving full-context performance. Further analyses suggest that CCOPD strengthens grounding in user evidence and reduces sensitivity to contamination from earlier assistant turns.

</details>


### [65] [How LoRA Remembers? A Parametric Memory Law for LLM Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30260)
*Ziwen Xu,Haiwen Hong,Linsong Yu,Benglei Cui,Longtao Huang,Hui Xue,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过将LoRA作为潜在空间中的可控记忆容量探测工具，量化了精确参数记忆能力。提出了参数记忆定律，揭示了损失减少与有效参数及序列长度之间的幂律关系；发现预测概率p＞0.5是贪婪解码下逐字回忆的充分条件；基于此提出了MemFT阈值引导优化策略，能提升记忆保真度和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需持续学习更新知识以适应动态现实环境。虽然低秩适应（LoRA）被广泛用于记忆更新，但现有研究主要依赖定性下游评估，缺乏对精确参数记忆的定量容量限制和潜在动态的深入探索。

Method: 将LoRA作为潜在空间中的可控记忆容量探测工具，系统量化精确参数记忆。提出参数记忆定律，建立损失减少ΔL与有效参数及序列长度之间的幂律关系。在token层面进行细粒度分析，发现预测概率p＞0.5是贪婪解码下逐字回忆的充分条件。基于此提出MemFT阈值引导优化策略，动态重新分配训练预算给亚阈值token。

Result: 实证评估表明，MemFT能显著提升记忆保真度和效率。参数记忆定律提供了稳健的幂律关系，揭示了参数记忆的定量特性。token级别的分析表明存在确定性相变，p＞0.5概率即为逐字回忆的充分条件。

Conclusion: 本研究通过量化分析揭示了LoRA参数记忆的定量特性，提出了参数记忆定律和MemFT优化策略，为LLM的持续学习和知识更新提供了新的理论框架和实用方法。代码将在https://github.com/zjunlp/ParametricMemoryLaw发布。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) must continuously learn and update knowledge to remain effective in dynamic real-world environments. While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is widely used for such memory updates, existing studies mainly rely on qualitative downstream evaluations, leaving the quantitative capacity limits and underlying dynamics of exact parametric memory largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we employ LoRA as a controlled memory capacity probe within the latent space to systematically quantify exact parametric memory. We introduce the Parametric Memory Law, a robust power law linking loss reduction Delta L to effective parameters and sequence length. At the token level, fine-grained analysis reveals a deterministic phase transition, demonstrating that a prediction probability of p > 0.5 constitutes a sufficient condition for verbatim recall under greedy decoding. Driven by these insights, we introduce MemFT, a threshold-guided optimization strategy that dynamically redistributes the training budget toward sub-threshold tokens. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that MemFT can enhance memory fidelity and efficiency. Code will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/ParametricMemoryLaw.

</details>

*Yutong Wang,Xuebo Liu,Derek F. Wong,Zhilin Li,Rongqing Jiang,Min Zhang,Shimin Tao,Daimeng Wei,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Document-level translation remains one of the most challenging tasks for large language models, which are constrained by limited context windows that impede global cohesion, while simultaneously suffering from redundant contextual information that degrades translation quality. To address this, we propose a human-like long document translation agent called Loong, which leverages a 3E memory module (Essence-Exemplar-Entity) to store summaries, sentence pairs, and entity records as historical context. Instead of passively attending to all history, Loong performs deep reasoning to adaptively identify the optimal context for translation guidance. Loong optimizes its context policy through reinforcement learning, utilizing preference data derived from its own sampled observe-and-act reasoning trajectories. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that Loong achieves substantial translation quality improvements in English $\Leftrightarrow$ Chinese, German, and French directions, with average gains of up to 13.0 points across the three evaluation metrics. Furthermore, Loong exhibits strong generalization across domains and robustness against contextual noise, while maintaining remarkable stability in ultra-long document translation. Our code is released at https://github.com/YutongWang1216/LoongDocMT.

</details>


### [67] [MedCase-Structured: A Text-to-FHIR Dataset for Benchmarking Diagnostic Reasoning in Clinically Realistic EHR Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30295)
*Valentina Bui Muti,Eugénie Dulout,Ziquan Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从非结构化文本生成 clinically realistic HL7 FHIR R4 bundles 的流程，用于控制评估临床决策支持系统。通过构建 MedCase-Structured 数据集，发现LLM在结构化FHIR输入上的诊断准确率低于纯文本输入，突出了部署对齐基准测试的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在临床推理和决策支持方面有潜力，但在现实、与电子健康记录一致的设置中评估有限。现有基准往往依赖静态数据集或非结构化输入，不反映临床系统使用的结构化、可互操作的数据格式（如HL7 FHIR）。

Method: 提出一个从非结构化文本生成 clinically realistic HL7 FHIR R4 bundles 的流程，结合分阶段LLM生成、基于术语的验证和修复，以减少幻觉代码并强制执行结构和语义一致性。应用于 MedCaseReasoning，构建 MedCase-Structured 数据集。

Result: MedCase-Structured 数据集实现了82.5%的有效FHIR生成率。评估显示LLM在结构化FHIR输入上的诊断准确率 consistently 低于纯文本输入，突出了与部署对齐的基准测试的重要性。

Conclusion: 强调在现实临床环境中使用结构化数据格式（如HL7 FHIR）进行LLM评估的必要性，以及部署对齐基准测试对于准确评估临床决策支持系统的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show promise for clinical reasoning and decision support, but evaluation in realistic, electronic health record-congruent settings remains limited. Existing benchmarks often rely on static datasets or unstructured inputs that do not reflect the structured, interoperable data formats used in clinical systems. We introduce a pipeline for generating clinically realistic HL7 FHIR R4 bundles from unstructured text, enabling controllable evaluation of clinical decision support systems. The pipeline combines staged LLM generation with terminology-grounded validation and repair to reduce hallucinated codes and enforce structural and semantic consistency. Applying this approach to MedCaseReasoning, we construct MedCase-Structured, a synthetic dataset aligned with clinician-authored diagnostic cases, achieving valid FHIR generation for 82.5% of cases. Evaluation on MedCase-Structured reveals consistently lower diagnostic accuracy for LLMs on structured FHIR inputs than with plain text, highlighting the importance of deployment-aligned benchmarking.

</details>


### [68] [Unlocking the Working Memory of Large Language Models for Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30343)
*Lukas Aichberger,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RiM采用固定记忆块替代自回归生成推理步骤，实现单次前向传播的高效潜在推理，在多个语言模型上达到或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时计算扩展方法依赖自回归生成中间token，将内部计算与外部通信混淆；人类认知可利用工作记忆在内部处理信息，无需外化中间想法。

Method: 提出Reasoning in Memory (RiM)：用固定序列的特殊token作为记忆块替代自回归推理步骤，解锁LLM工作记忆容量；采用两阶段课程学习：1)预测显式推理步骤进行接地；2)移除步骤级监督，迭代优化最终答案。

Result: 在推理基准测试中，RiM在各类语言模型家族和大小上均匹配或超越现有潜在推理方法。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可以被训练为使用工作记忆作为有效的潜在推理机制，实现无需自回归生成的计算高效推理。

Abstract: To improve the reasoning capabilities of large language models, test-time compute is typically scaled by generating intermediate tokens before the final answer. However, this couples reasoning to autoregressive generation and thereby conflates internal computation with external communication. In contrast, human cognition can use working memory to hold and manipulate information internally without the need to externalize intermediate thoughts. Drawing on this principle, we introduce Reasoning in Memory (RiM), a latent reasoning method that replaces the autoregressive generation of reasoning steps with memory blocks. These memory blocks are fixed sequences of special tokens that unlock the working-memory capacity of large language models. Since they are fixed rather than generated, they can be processed in a single forward pass, enabling compute-efficient latent reasoning. To operationalize these memory blocks, we employ a two-stage curriculum. First, we ground them by predicting explicit reasoning steps after each memory block. Second, we discard this step-level supervision and iteratively refine the final answer after each memory block. Our experiments on reasoning benchmarks show that, across language models of different families and sizes, RiM matches or exceeds existing latent reasoning methods while avoiding the autoregressive generation of thoughts. These results demonstrate that large language models can be trained to use working memory as an effective mechanism for latent reasoning.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [69] [Mechanistic origins of catastrophic forgetting: why RL preserves circuits better than SFT?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28860)
*Jeanmely Rojas Nunez,Viraj Sawant,Nathan Allen,Nomgondalai Amgalanbaatar,Yannis Zongo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究对比强化学习(RL)与监督微调(SFT)在保持大语言模型内部计算电路方面的差异，发现RL能更好地保留基础电路，从而减少灾难性遗忘，但任务适应速度较慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型微调常导致灾难性遗忘，近期研究表明RL比SFT能更好地保留先验能力，但尚不清楚这是否源于RL更好地保持了内部计算电路。

Method: 引入"差分电路脆弱性"作为头级别的电路退化度量指标，在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct模型上比较RL和SFT在科学问答任务中的表现。

Result: 发现明确的机制权衡：SFT能更快适应目标任务，但导致更大的电路破坏和先验能力遗忘；RL能保留更多基础电路，但适应速度较慢。

Conclusion: 电路保存可能是RL对灾难性遗忘更具鲁棒性的机制解释。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) frequently induces catastrophic forgetting of prior capabilities. Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning (RL) retains prior capabilities more effectively than supervised fine-tuning (SFT), attributing this to policy-gradient updates remaining closer to the base policy \cite{shenfeld2025rl}. We extend this behavioral account to the mechanistic level and ask whether RL's advantage is mirrored by stronger preservation of internal computational circuits. We introduce differential circuit vulnerability, a head-level measure of how much a circuit degrades under fine-tuning, and use it to compare RL and SFT on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct adapted to scientific question-answering. We find a clear mechanistic trade-off: SFT adapts more rapidly to the target task but produces substantially greater circuit disruption and forgetting of prior capabilities, whereas RL preserves a larger fraction of the base circuit at the cost of slower task adaptation. These findings suggest that circuit preservation may help explain why RL is more robust to catastrophic forgetting. We released our code here: https://github.com/rl-sft-circuit-research/differential-circuit-vulnerability.

</details>


### [70] [Continuity and Ordinality Matter: Constraining Time Series Tokens for Effective Time Series Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28866)
*Musheng Li,Ziying Zhang,Cheng jin,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Abstract: Token-based time series large language models (TS-LLMs) have emerged as a promising direction for time series analysis and reasoning. However, prior studies largely overlook the inherent continuity and ordinality of time series tokens, which substantially limits model performance. In this paper, we argue that preserving these properties in time series token embeddings is crucial for the effectiveness of token-based TS-LLMs. To this end, we propose COM (Continuity and Ordinality Matter), a continuity- and ordinality-aware strategy that integrates geometric constraints into both the initialization and training stages. Empirical results on multiple time series analysis benchmarks demonstrate that COM consistently improves the performance of token-based TS-LLMs, achieving competitive results and strong generalizability. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/COM .

</details>


### [71] [Representation Alignment Rests on Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28870)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过信号、偏差和噪声的三元统计框架研究Platonic表征假设（PRH），提出线性表征假设（LRH）解释PRH，发现稀疏自编码器提取的线性特征具有更强的跨模态对齐性，并提出统计模型综合解释现代AI架构中表征对齐的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Platonic表征假设（PRH）在多元AI架构中的表现，探索表征对齐的内在机制，理解不同模型间表征对齐的异同及其成因。

Method: 构建包含信号、偏差和噪声的三元统计框架：1）信号方面提出线性表征假设（LRH），使用稀疏自编码器提取线性对象-属性特征；2）偏差方面分析不同架构和训练过程的隐式偏差，提出居中和归一化作为缓解方法；3）噪声方面研究有限样本训练导致的表征噪声，发现词频与对齐性的正相关关系。

Result: 稀疏自编码器提取的线性特征表现出比密集表示更强的跨模态对齐性；居中和归一化能部分缓解不同模型的偏差差异；表征噪声与数据稀缺相关，在LLM和文本嵌入模型中观察到词频与对齐性存在强正相关；提出的统计模型成功解释了PRH及相关表征对齐现象。

Conclusion: 线性表征假设（LRH）为理解Platonic表征假设提供了有力解释，通过稀疏表征提取、偏差缓解和噪声分析的三元框架，揭示了现代AI架构中表征对齐的本质规律，为跨模型表征对齐提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) through a tripartite statistical framework of representations: signal, bias, and noise. {1) Signal:} We propose that Platonic alignment arises from the universal relationship between objects and attributes, which is encoded linearly in representations according to the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH). We provide evidence that LRH helps explain PRH by extracting linear object-attribute features with sparse autoencoders and showing that these sparse representations often exhibit stronger cross-modal alignment than their dense counterparts. {2) Bias:} Models have different implicit biases due to the diverse architectures and training procedures used. We show that this difference can be partially mitigated. Centering and normalization consistently improve cross-model alignment. {3) Noise:} Finite-sample training leads to noise in representations. We provide evidence that representational noise is driven by data scarcity by revealing a strong and consistent positive correlation between word frequency and alignment in LLMs and text embedding models. Synthesizing signal, bias, and noise, we propose a statistical model that refines the Linear Representation Hypothesis and explains further phenomena related to the alignment of representations emerging from diverse modern AI architectures.

</details>


### [72] [When LLM Reward Design Fails: Diagnostic-Driven Refinement for Sparse Structured RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28918)
*Youting Wang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu,Xuan Liu,Dingyan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对稀疏结构化RL任务，研究证明LLM奖励函数生成更适合作为调试而非一次性生成，提出基于诊断驱动的迭代优化方法，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM一次性生成的奖励函数存在奖励泛滥和语义/API理解错误等失败模式，需探索迭代调试方法是否优于一次性生成。

Method: 提出诊断驱动的迭代优化框架，使用训练诊断和失败模式分类指导奖励函数修订，在MiniGrid（稀疏任务）和MuJoCo（边界压力测试）上进行评估，采用多种控制实验（重试、静态词汇、预算匹配等）分离优化与选择/训练效应。

Result: DoorKey-8x8性能从2.3%提升至97.6%，KeyCorridor从31.2%提升至86.7%；控制实验表明改进源于调试方法而非重试或额外训练，静态词汇控制恢复大部分性能差距；但在密集奖励运动任务中，基于成功的诊断可能失效，回报趋势反馈可移除一个假阳性机制但无稳健提升。

Conclusion: LLM奖励函数生成更适合框架化为调试而非一次性生成，成功诊断在稀疏结构化任务有效，但在密集奖励运动任务存在边界限制；该方法成本低于基于人群的奖励搜索，但适用范围限于PPO框架下具有可靠接口的稀疏结构化任务。

Abstract: For sparse, structured reinforcement-learning tasks with semantic reward-function interfaces, LLM-generated reward shaping is better framed as debugging than one-shot generation. We study PPO-trained agents using MiniGrid as core evaluation and MuJoCo as boundary stress test. Our audit finds two dominant one-shot failure modes -- reward flooding and semantic/API misunderstanding -- plus a rarer weak-shaping case. We propose diagnostic-driven iterative refinement, where training diagnostics and a failure-mode taxonomy guide targeted reward-function revision. Refinement improves DoorKey-8x8 from 2.3% to 97.6% and KeyCorridor from 31.2% to 86.7% with high seed-to-seed variance. Controls show these gains are not from retrying or extra training: metrics-only re-prompting yields large drops, while a static-vocabulary control recovers much of the gap (87.6%; 70.7%), showing the taxonomy prompt is a major mechanism and dynamic labels provide only partially isolated incremental evidence. Budget-matched and Best-of-3 comparisons separate refinement from selection and training-time effects. Component-removal tests, sensitivity analyses, and an audit against author labels provide converging evidence for the debugging interpretation while revealing calibration limits. Continuous-control results show the boundary: success-based diagnostics can misfire in dense-reward locomotion, and return-trend feedback removes one false-positive mechanism without robust gains. The low-call protocol is a cost contrast with population-based reward search, not a benchmark comparison. In four crossed-variance-design environments, point estimates suggest larger gains when LLM reward-function variance dominates but bootstrap intervals are wide. The method is bounded to sparse structured tasks with reliable interfaces under PPO; fields like event_text may help, hurt, or be neutral.

</details>


### [73] [CosmicFish-HRM: Adaptive Reasoning via Hierarchical Recurrent Mechanisms in Compact Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28919)
*Venkat Akhil Lakkapragada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CosmicFish-HRM模型，通过分层推理模块动态调整推理深度，在参数量较小的模型中实现自适应推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然推理能力强，但需要大量参数和昂贵计算。研究探索在紧凑模型中通过自适应推理深度替代单纯依赖参数规模来实现推理能力。

Method: 构建基于分层推理模块（HRM）的紧凑语言模型CosmicFish-HRM，采用分组查询注意力、RoPE和SwiGLU激活函数。模型在推理过程中动态分配计算资源，通过高低级推理循环并根据输入复杂度学习何时停止。

Result: 模型成功学习了非均匀推理行为，能够根据任务和输入分配不同数量的推理步骤。虽然小型模型中推理基础设施增加了开销，但随着模型规模增大，这种权衡变得更加有利。

Conclusion: 自适应推理深度可能为推理能力提供一种有前景的替代方案，不再完全依赖参数规模来获得推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved strong reasoning capabilities, though often at the cost of massive parameter counts and expensive inference. In this work, we explore a different direction: adaptive reasoning depth in compact language models. We present CosmicFish-HRM, a compact language model built around a Hierarchical Reasoning Module (HRM) that dynamically allocates computational effort during inference. Instead of applying fixed computation to every input, the model iterates through high-level and low-level reasoning cycles and learns when to halt based on input complexity. CosmicFish-HRM combines this adaptive reasoning core with modern transformer components including Grouped Query Attention, RoPE, and SwiGLU activations. While the additional reasoning infrastructure introduces overhead at small scale, we hypothesize that this tradeoff becomes increasingly favorable as model size grows and the relative cost of the HRM core diminishes. Our results show that the model learns non-uniform reasoning behavior, allocating different numbers of reasoning steps across tasks and inputs. These findings suggest that adaptive reasoning depth may offer a promising alternative to relying solely on parameter scale for reasoning capability.

</details>


### [74] [Label-Free Reinforcement Learning via Cross-Model Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29009)
*Matt Gorbett,Hossein Shirazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出跨模型熵(CME)作为大语言模型RL后训练的标签无关奖励信号，通过独立验证模型评估生成响应的似然性来提供连续、无需训练的奖励信号，在开放性指令跟随任务上显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练的瓶颈在于奖励信号。现有方法需要真值验证奖励（限制在数学、代码等自动检查领域）或人类偏好标签（昂贵且易受奖励黑客攻击）。标签无关方法使用自参考信号但存在强化自身错误的风险。

Method: 提出跨模型熵(CME)作为标签无关奖励信号：定义为生成器响应在独立验证模型下的平均对数似然。CME是连续的、无需训练的，基于"验证模型认为不惊讶的响应很可能是正确或高质量的"原则。由于验证器与生成器独立，信号无法通过自洽性被作弊。CME集成到GRPO中，无需改变训练循环。

Result: 在开放性指令跟随任务（UltraFeedback提示词，AlpacaEval 2.0评估）上，CME奖励在四类模型家族（Qwen、Llama、Gemma、OLMo）和三种训练模式（预训练、SFT、指令微调）中均击败未训练基线，在LLM-as-Judge两两比较中，调整平局后的胜率范围从52.5%到71.4%。

Conclusion: 代码将在论文发表时发布。CME为开放性指令跟随等不适合自参考信号的任务提供了有效的标签无关RL训练方法。

Abstract: Post-training large language models with reinforcement learning is bottlenecked by the reward signal. Existing approaches require either ground-truth verifiable rewards, restricting training to domains with automatic correctness checks (e.g., mathematics, code execution), or human preference labels, which are expensive to collect and prone to reward hacking. Recent label-free methods replace ground-truth verifiers with self-referential signals like majority voting or token entropy over a model's own outputs, but risk reinforcing a model's own errors. In this work we propose Cross-Model Entropy (CME), the mean log-likelihood of a generator's response under a separate verifier model, as a label-free reward signal for RL post-training. CME is continuous, training-free, and grounded in the principle that responses a verifier finds unsurprising are likely correct or high quality. Because the verifier is independent of the generator, the signal cannot be gamed through self-consistency. We integrate CME into GRPO with no other changes to the training loop, extending label-free RL to open-ended instruction following -- a regime where self-referential signals are inapplicable or poorly suited. On open-ended instruction following (UltraFeedback prompts, evaluated on AlpacaEval 2.0), CME rewards beat the untrained base in head-to-head LLM-as-Judge comparisons across four model families (Qwen, Llama, Gemma, OLMo) and three training regimes (pretrained, SFT, and instruction-tuned), with tie-adjusted win rates ranging from 52.5% to 71.4%. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [75] [RUBRIC-ARROW: Alternating Pointwise Rubric Reward Modeling for LLM Post-training in Non-verifiable Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29156)
*Haoxiang Jiang,Zihan Dong,Tianci Liu,Wanying Wang,Ran Xu,Tony Yu,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RUBRIC-ARROW是一种交替训练框架，通过联合训练评分标准和条件裁判员来改进LLM后训练的点式奖励建模，使用概率评分规则减少平局，仅依赖成对偏好数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点式奖励建模在主观、非验证设置中难以进行绝对评分；现有基于评分标准的方法依赖前沿LLM且容易出现平局。

Method: 提出交替框架RUBRIC-ARROW，联合训练评分标准生成器和条件裁判员，使用概率评分规则减少平局，结合阶段特定偏好奖励和交替GRPO方案。

Result: 在大量实验中达到竞争性的奖励建模准确率，并为下游策略后训练带来一致的增益。

Conclusion: RUBRIC-ARROW通过交替训练和概率评分有效解决了评分标准和裁判中的平局问题，在奖励建模准确性和下游策略训练中均表现良好。

Abstract: Pointwise reward modeling offers critical signals for LLM post-training, yet struggles with absolute scoring in subjective, non-verifiable settings. Rubric-based methods address this by decomposing evaluation into explicit criteria, but existing approaches typically depend on frontier LLMs and suffer from ties caused by hard Boolean aggregation. We present RUBRIC-ARROW, an alternating framework that jointly trains a rubric generator and a rubric-conditioned judge, with its RL stage using only pairwise preference data. Our method couples a probability-based scoring rule that reduces ties with phase-specific preference-based rewards and an alternating GRPO scheme that together train the pointwise evaluator. Extensive experiments show that RUBRIC-ARROW achieves competitive reward-modeling accuracy and yields consistent gains for downstream policy post-training.

</details>


### [76] [Influence-Guided Symbolic Regression: Scientific Discovery via LLM-Driven Equation Search with Granular Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29184)
*Evgeny S. Saveliev,Samuel Holt,Nabeel Seedat,David L. Bentley,Jim Weatherall,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IGSR通过细粒度的影响分数引导LLM进行符号回归，将方程发现视为迭代的两步过程（生成候选基函数+基于影响分数的严格选择），显著提高了发现效率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在科学发现中具有潜力，但在符号回归中的应用受到低效搜索策略和粗糙反馈信号的制约。当前方法通常使用标量指标（如全局均方误差）来指导，无法识别方程中哪些成分驱动性能或导致误差。

Method: IGSR将方程发现框架化为迭代的两步过程：1) LLM生成候选基函数ψ_j(x)用于线性模型；2) 使用细粒度影响分数Δ_j评估每个术语，量化其对泛化精度的边际贡献，实现基于影响的剪枝过程。将此机制与蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)集成，在组合搜索空间中平衡探索新颖函数形式与利用高影响成分。

Result: 在LLM-SRBench、药代动力学PKPD模型、流行病学模拟和真实基因组数据等多种基准上验证了IGSR的有效性。在包含DNA甲基化和RNA聚合酶II暂停关系的高维生物数据案例研究中，IGSR发现了一个新颖的关系，并通过湿实验得到验证。

Conclusion: IGSR通过细粒度影响分数引导LLM进行方程发现，相比传统标量指标能更准确地识别驱动性能或导致误差的方程成分，实现了真正的科学发现，证明了该方法在符号回归任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for scientific discovery, yet their application to symbolic regression is often constrained by inefficient search strategies and coarse feedback signals. Current methods typically guide LLMs using scalar metrics (e.g., global Mean Squared Error), which fail to identify which components of a proposed equation are driving performance or causing error. We introduce \textit{Influence-Guided Symbolic Regression} (IGSR), a method that frames equation discovery as an iterative two-step process combining diverse term generation with rigorous selection: an LLM generates candidate basis functions $ψ_j(\mathbf{x})$ for a linear model, which are then evaluated using granular influence scores $Δ_j$. These scores quantify each term's marginal contribution to generalization accuracy, enabling an influence-guided pruning process that systematically refines the model structure. Integrating this mechanism into a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) enables navigating the combinatorial search space while balancing exploration of novel functional forms with exploitation of high-influence components. We demonstrate IGSR's effectiveness on a diverse suite of benchmarks, including LLM-SRBench, pharmacological PKPD models, an epidemiological simulation, and real-world genomic data. Notably, we validate the framework's capacity for genuine discovery in a case study using a high-dimensional biological dataset, in which IGSR identified a novel relationship between DNA methylation and RNA Polymerase II pausing; a hypothesis that was subsequently supported via wet-lab experimentation.

</details>


### [77] [When RL Suppresses Its Own Vocabulary: Recovering Reasoning Diversity in Puzzle-to-Math Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29190)
*Mayug Maniparambil,Arjun Karuvally,Terrence Sejnowski,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了RLVR在LLM跨领域推理中的迁移机制。发现基于谜题的SFT训练能生成可迁移的推理原语，但传统RL会抑制探索性推理。通过引入新颖性奖励机制恢复被抑制的推理原语，最终在未添加数学问题的情况下将硬数学问题能力从16.0%提升至36.0%，总增益达20pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR能提升LLM推理能力，但其在跨领域迁移的条件和机制尚不明确。需要理解：1）推理原语如何从谜题训练迁移到数学问题；2）RL阶段为何会抑制探索性推理；3）如何设计训练策略以最大化跨领域迁移效果。

Method: 1. 使用仅包含约束满足谜题的7B模型进行SFT和RL训练；2. 引入推理原语级框架，结合9类span分类器和motif提取，将思维链分割为原语并追踪其在训练各阶段和领域的演化；3. 对比vanilla GSPO与引入新颖性奖励（基于参考模型困惑度奖励多样化正确rollouts）的版本；4. 分析探索性原语（如hypothesize和backtrack）在RL阶段的变化。

Result: 1. 谜题SFT诱导出推理原语词汇，在OlymMATH-Hard上带来+7pp pass@32增益；2. Vanilla GSPO将这些原语组合成长计算验证链，再带来+6pp增益；3. Vanilla RL抑制探索性原语；4. 新颖性奖励恢复这些原语，再带来+7pp增益；5. 端到端方法在不添加数学问题的情况下，将硬数学能力从16.0%提升至36.0%，总增益20pp。

Conclusion: 谜题训练可以生成可迁移到数学问题的推理原语，但传统RL会抑制探索性推理。通过新颖性奖励机制恢复这些原语是提升跨领域迁移的关键。该方法在不添加数学训练数据的情况下实现了显著性能提升，证明了推理原语迁移的可行性和重要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning using verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves LLM reasoning, but the conditions under which it transfers across domains -- and why it does so -- remain under-explored. We study cross-domain transfer in a 7B model whose SFT and RL post-training stages use only constraint-satisfaction puzzles, with no mathematics problems in the post-training data. To analyze how transfer emerges, we introduce a reasoning primitive-level framework that combines a 9-class span classifier with motif extraction, allowing us to segment chain-of-thought traces into primitive motifs and track their evolution across training stages and domains. We find that puzzle SFT induces a reasoning-primitive vocabulary, yielding a $+7$pp \texttt{pass@32} gain on OlymMATH-Hard. Vanilla GSPO then composes these primitives into longer compute-verify chains, adding a further $+6$pp. However, this RL stage also suppresses exploratory primitives such as \textit{hypothesize} and \textit{backtrack}. To address this, we introduce a novelty bonus that rewards diverse correct rollouts, using perplexity under the reference model as a signal. This restores recovery primitives during RL and adds a further $+7$pp \texttt{pass@32} relative to vanilla GSPO. Finally, the end-to-end recipe raises the hard-math capability ceiling from $16.0\%$ at the OLMo3-7B-Instruct-SFT base to $36.0\%$, without adding any mathematics problems during the SFT or RL stages.

</details>


### [78] [Inferring the Size of Large Language Models From Popular Text Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29223)
*Ivica Nikolic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种仅基于模型输出的黑盒方法，通过分析其对经典文本的预测准确度来推断LLM的保守参数下限，无需访问模型内部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM开发者的参数数量通常被保密，而参数数量是评估模型能力和成本的关键指标。现有的公开信息不足以了解模型规模，需要一种从外部推断的方法。

Method: 核心观察：流行经典文本（文学、宗教文本、基础文件）几乎存在于所有大规模预训练语料库中。方法步骤：1）收集模型对多种经典文本片段的next-token预测；2）构建准确度特征向量；3）使用两种推断方法：成对统计测试（比较两个模型大小）和PCA缩放定律估计器（提取潜在索引映射到参数数量）。

Result: 在广泛的开源模型上验证，两种方法都产生准确可靠的保守下限。应用于闭源模型时，框架恢复了内部产品层次结构，揭示了行业缩放策略的分歧（有些开发者有显著参数增长，有些存在严格参数上限）。

Conclusion: 即使存在严格的API限制，也能系统地探测隐藏的设计选择。该框架证明了可以通过外部行为推断模型内部设计，为理解LLM开发实践提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The parameter counts of the most widely used large language models (LLMs) are often withheld by their developers, leaving model size -- a primary reference point for interpreting capabilities and costs -- largely undisclosed. We propose a black-box method to infer conservative lower bounds on LLM size from generated text outputs alone, requiring nothing beyond the ability to submit text fragments and observe next-token predictions. Our approach is grounded in a key observation: popular, widely-circulated texts -- such as classical literature, religious texts, and foundational documents -- are present in virtually every large-scale pretraining corpus, and how accurately a model predicts the next word across text fragments of varying length is a reliable signal of how much it has memorized them, which in turn is fundamentally limited by its total parameter count. We aggregate this memorization signal across a diverse corpus of texts and fragment lengths into a single accuracy profile vector per model, and build two complementary inference methods on top of it: a pairwise statistical test that determines which of two models is larger, and a scaling-law estimator that extracts a one-dimensional latent index from these vectors via Principal Component Analysis (PCA) to map the aggregated signal to a parameter count. Validated on a broad set of open-weight models, both methods produce accurate and reliable lower bounds. When applied to popular closed-weight models, our framework recovers internal product hierarchies and reveals a clear divergence in industry scaling strategies: while some developers yield significantly higher bounds indicative of large generational parameter growth, others operate under strict parameter ceilings, demonstrating that hidden design choices can be systematically probed even under strict API limitations.

</details>


### [79] [BlockBatch: Multi-Scale Consensus Decoding for Efficient Diffusion Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29233)
*Xiaoyou Wu,Cheng-Jhih Shih,Binfei Ji,Yong Liu,Yingyan,Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BlockBatch提出一种训练无关的在线推理框架，通过并行执行多个块大小的分支来优化扩散语言模型的推理速度，平均减少26.6%的NFEs并实现1.33×的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在块-wise推理中面临块大小的权衡难题：小块保留局部条件但需要更多去噪步骤，大面积块提供更多并行性但可能导致过早承诺和缓存误差。现有加速方法通常为每个请求选择单一块大小，未利用不同块大小之间的互补性。

Method: BlockBatch是一个训练无关的在线推理框架，通过以下机制协调多个块大小分支：(1)置信度门控的token合并；(2)基于领导者同步的协调机制；(3)周期性的全序列刷新以重新锚定局部块更新到全局一致的KV状态。不同块大小分支共享初始前缀，在语义决定位置分歧，随后在语法轻量级token上达成一致。

Result: 在3个代表性扩散语言模型和4个数据集上，BlockBatch平均减少去噪NFEs 26.6%，端到端速度比Fast-dLLM快1.33×，同时保持准确率。

Conclusion: 块大小多样性是一个实用且此前被充分探索的分支并行扩散语言模型推理轴。BlockBatch证明了并行执行相关但非相同块大小分支的有效性，为扩散语言模型的推理加速提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising multiple token positions in parallel, offering an attractive alternative to strictly autoregressive decoding. In practice, however, block-wise dLLM inference exposes a difficult granularity trade-off: small blocks preserve local conditioning but require many denoising steps, whereas large blocks expose more parallelism but can make premature commitments and accumulate cache error. Existing acceleration methods typically choose a single block size per request, leaving the complementarity among block sizes unused. We show that block size itself is a useful branching dimension. Different block sizes induce related but non-identical KV-cache trajectories: branches often share an initial prefix, bifurcate at semantically decisive positions, and later agree on syntactically lightweight tokens. Motivated by this structure, we propose BlockBatch, a training-free online inference framework that executes multiple block-size branches for the same request inside a batched forward pass. BlockBatch coordinates these branches through confidence-gated token merging, leader-based synchronization, and periodic full-sequence refreshes that re-anchor local block updates to a globally consistent KV state. Across 3 representative dLLMs and 4 datasets, BlockBatch reduces denoising NFEs by 26.6\% on average and achieves a 1.33$\times$ average end-to-end speedup over Fast-dLLM while preserving accuracy. These results identify block-size diversity as a practical and previously underexplored axis for branch-parallel dLLM inference.

</details>


### [80] [GDSD: Reinforcement Learning as Guided Denoiser Self-Distillation for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29398)
*Xiaohang Tang,Keyue Jiang,Che Liu,Qifang Zhao,Xiaoxiao Xu,Sangwoong Yoon,Ilija Bogunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Guided Denoiser Self-Distillation (GDSD)方法，通过直接蒸馏dLLM的去噪器来提升性能，避免ELBO替代似然度带来的偏差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可改进扩散大语言模型(dLLMs)的策略，但策略似然度难以计算。现有基于ELBO的方法虽有效，但因训练-推理不匹配引入偏差，导致性能下降

Method: GDSD从反向KL正则化RL的闭式最优解中推导出优势引导的自教师，通过无归一化目标将dLLM的去噪器logits与教师匹配，实现无ELBO似然替代的蒸馏

Result: 在LLaDA-8B和Dream-7B模型上，GDSD在规划、数学和编码基准测试中一致优于现有ELBO方法，测试准确率提升最高达+19.6%，训练奖励动态更稳定

Conclusion: 直接去噪器自蒸馏、无需ELBO似然替代，可为dLLMs提供更稳定有效的强化学习方案

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can be used to improve the policy (denoiser) of diffusion large language models (dLLMs), while being hindered by the intractability of the policy likelihood. A dominant and efficient family of methods replaces the likelihood in standard RL with its evidence lower bound (ELBO), estimated from randomly masked sequences. Despite being well aligned with pre-training, these approaches introduce bias through training--inference mismatch by using the ELBO as a likelihood surrogate, which can degrade performance. In this work, we propose Guided Denoiser Self-Distillation (GDSD) to directly distill the denoiser of dLLMs from an advantage-guided self-teacher, derived from the closed-form optimum of reverse-KL regularized RL. GDSD matches the dLLM's denoiser logits to the teacher's via a normalization-free objective, which reduces RL to likelihood-free self-distillation and thus bypasses the TIM biases. Recent ELBO-based methods emerge as instances of applying different distillation divergences, but with diagnosable pathologies that GDSD avoids. On planning, math, and coding benchmarks with LLaDA-8B and Dream-7B, GDSD consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art ELBO-based methods with a more stable training reward dynamics, achieving test-accuracy improvements of up to $+19.6\%$. These results suggest that direct denoiser self-distillation, without relying on an ELBO likelihood surrogate, can provide a more stable and effective RL procedure for dLLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/GaryBall/GDSD.

</details>
